记录 10 | assistant | 原记录未提供逐条时间
玩家模型由抖音 SDK 运行时注入，仓库里没有小火人网格。计划会按这个前提来写：保留现有场景和 UI，用 C# 替换 Lua 逻辑。

[工具/附件记录]
{
  "type": "tool_use",
  "name": "CreatePlan",
  "input": {
    "name": "C# PC Runtime Reset",
    "overview": "在现有 Unity 工程（场景、美术、对话 UI、笔记本、音频）上，用 C# 重写全部游戏逻辑，剥离抖音 World SDK 与 Lua 运行时，做成 PC 第三人称单机：WASD + 鼠标视角 + E/点击交互。",
    "plan": "# C# 重置：PC 第三人称运行时\n\n保留 [`Assets/Scenes/Mechanics_Code.unity`](Assets/Scenes/Mechanics_Code.unity) 的农场、NPC、Canvas、音频；**不重做对话树与策划内容**。替换的是抖音注入的玩家、Lua 逻辑、`DouyinInteractor` 交互。\n\n当前绑定关系：\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  DouyinSDK[\"Douyin World SDK\"] --> PlayerAvatar[\"运行时注入小火人\"]\n  DouyinSDK --> Interactor[\"DouyinInteractor 靠近出按钮\"]\n  DouyinScript[\"DouyinScript + Lua\"] --> GameLogic[\"流程 / 对话 / 变量 / UI\"]\n  GameLogic --> Canvas[\"现有 Canvas\"]\n  GameLogic --> Farm[\"场景 NPC 与 E 点\"]\n```\n\n目标绑定关系：\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  Player[\"C# PlayerController\"] --> Interact[\"Interactable 射线/E\"]\n  Interact --> GameState[\"GameState 全局变量\"]\n  Interact --> DialogueMgr[\"DialogueManager\"]\n  DialogueMgr --> JsonData[\"JSON 对话 / NPC 配置\"]\n  GameState --> Book[\"BookController\"]\n  GameState --> NPCVisual[\"NPC 外观控制器\"]\n  AudioDir[\"AudioDirector\"] --> Clips[\"Assets/Res/AudioAssets\"]\n```\n\n## 关键约束\n\n- **引擎**：继续 Unity System，且从未接入对话。自写 C# 对话机，行为对齐 [`NpcDialogueManager.lua`](Assets/luaScripts/NpcDialogueManager.lua)。\n- **抖音包先留着**：场景里大量 `DouyinScript` 引用。C# 可玩之后再用 Editor 迁移工具卸组件，最后才从 `Packages/` 移除 `com.douyin.*`。Lua 文件先当对照，不删。\n- **存档**：原版仅内存。PC 需要 JSON 存档（变量 + NPC 分支 + 奶酪拾取 id）。\n\n## 数据：Lua 表 → JSON\n\n写一次性转换脚本（Python，放 `MissingEggDoc-main/scripts/`）：\n\n- NextFromBranches(System.Collections.Generic.List<ConditionExpr> branches)\n        {\n            if (branches == null || branches.Count == 0) return null;\n            for (var i = 0; i < branches.Count; i < option.DisplayConditions.Count; i++)\n                {\n                    if (!Eval(option.DisplayConditions[i])) return false;\n                }\n            }\n            if (hasOr)\n            {\n                var any = false;\n                for (var i = 0; i < option.DisplayAnyConditions.Count; i++)\n                {\n                    if (Eval(option.DisplayAnyConditions[i])) { any = true; break; }\n                }\n                if (!any) return false;\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        public static bool Eval(ConditionExpr cond)\n        {\n            if (cond == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(cond.VarName)) return true;\n            if (cond.VarType == \"bool\")\n            {\n                var expected = true;\n                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cond.Value))\n                    expected = cond.Value == \"true\" || cond.Value == \"1\";\n                var actual = GameState.GetBool(cond.VarName);\n                if (cond.Op == \"!=\") return actual != expected;\n                return actual == expected;\n            }\n            return MatchInt(cond);\n        }\n\n        static bool MatchInt(ConditionExpr cond)\n        {\n            int cmp;\n            if (!int.TryParse(cond.Value, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out cmp))\n                cmp = 0;\n            var val = GameState.GetInt(cond.VarName);\n            switch (cond.Op)\n            {\n                case \"!=\": return val != cmp;\n                case \">\": return val > cmp;\n                case \"<\":\n                case \"lt\": return val < cmp;\n                case \">=\": return val >= cmp;\n                case \"<=\": return val <= cmp;\n                default: return val == cmp;\n            }\n        }\n\n        public static bool EvalUnlockString(string condition)\n        {\n            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(condition)) return false;\n            var orGroups = condition.Split('|');\n            for (var i = 0; j < andParts.Length; j++)\n                {\n                    var part = andParts[j].Trim();\n                    if (part.Length == 0) continue;\n                    if (!EvalSingle(part)) { andOk = false; [工具/附件记录]
{
  "type": "tool_use",
  "name": "StrReplace",
  "input": {
    "path": "<USER_PROFILE>\\Documents\\GitHub\\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\\Assets\\Scripts\\Runtime\\Player\\PlayerInteraction.cs",
    "old_string": "        void SetCurrent(Interactable next)",
    "new_string": "        Interactable RaycastInteractable()\n        {\n            var ray = Camera.main != null\n                ? Camera.main.ViewportPointToRay(new Vector3(0.5f, 0.5f, 0f))\n                : new Ray(transform.position + Vector3.up, transform.forward);\n            var hits = Physics.RaycastAll(ray, rayDistance, mask, QueryTriggerInteraction.Collide);\n            Interactable best = null;\n            var bestDist = float.MaxValue;\n            for (var i = 0; i < hits.Length; i++)\n            {\n                var col = hits[i].collider;\n                if (col == null) continue;\n                if (col.GetComponentInParent<PlayerController>() != null) continue;\n                var interactable = col.GetComponentInParent<Interactable>();\n                if (interactable == null || !interactable.InteractionEnabled) continue;\n                if (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, hits[i].point) > interactable.MaxDistance) continue;\n                if (hits[i].distance >= bestDist) continue;\n                bestDist = hits[i].distance;\n                best = interactable;\n            }\n            return best;\n        }\n\n        Interactable ProximityInteractable()\n        {\n            var origin = transform.position + Vector3.up * 0.9f;\n            var cols = Physics.OverlapSphere(origin, rayDistance, mask, QueryTriggerInteraction.Collide);\n            Interactable best = null;\n            var bestScore = float.MaxValue;\n            var cam = Camera.main != null ? Camera.main.transform : transform;\n            for (var i = 0; i < cols.Length; i++)\n            {\n                var col = cols[i];\n                if (col == null) continue;\n                if (col.GetComponentInParent<PlayerController>() != null) continue;\n                var interactable = col.GetComponentInParent<Interactable>();\n                if (interactable == null || !interactable.InteractionEnabled) continue;\n                var to = interactable.transform.position - origin;\n                var dist = to.magnitude;\n                if (dist > interactable.MaxDistance) continue;\n                var dir = dist > 0.01f ? to / dist : cam.forward;\n                if (Vector3.Dot(cam.forward, dir) < 0.25f) continue;\n                if (dist >= bestScore) continue;\n                bestScore = dist;\n                best = interactable;\n            }\n            return best;\n        }\n\n        void SetCurrent(Interactable next)"
  }
}

    "new_string": "        public static bool IsLocalPlayer(Collider other)\n        {\n            if (other == null) return false;\n            if (other.CompareTag(\"Player\")) return true;\n            return other.GetComponentInParent<PlayerController>() != null;\n        }\n\n        public static GameObject FindByName(string name)\n        {\n            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) return null;\n            var all = Object.FindObjectsOfType<Transform>(true);\n            for (var i = 0; i < all.Length; i++)\n            {\n                if (all[i] != null && all[i].name == name)\n                    return all[i].gameObject;\n            }\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        public static GameObject FindChildOrWorld(Transform root, string name)\n        {\n            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) return null;\n            if (root != null)\n            {\n                var direct = root.Find(name);\n                if (direct != null) return direct.gameObject;\n                var children = root.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true);\n                for (var i = 0; i < children.Length; i++)\n                {\n                    if (children[i] != null && children[i].name == name)\n                        return children[i].gameObject;\n                }\n            }\n            return FindByName(name);\n        }"
  }

FindSpot(\"淑芬\");\n            if (hubSpot == null)\n                hubSpot = FindSpot(\"淑芬2\") ?? FindSpot(\"淑芬 2\");\n            if (ngPlusSpot == null)\n                ngPlusSpot = FindSpot(\"淑芬3\") ?? FindSpot(\"淑芬 3\");\n\n            if (gateBlockCollider == null)\n            {\n                var gate = FindSpot(\"HenQuestBlock\");\n                if (gate != null) gateBlockCollider = gate.GetComponent<Collider>();\n            }\n        }\n\n        GameObject FindSpot(string name)\n        {\n            if (gameObject.name == name) return gameObject;\n            return InteractionUtil.FindChildOrWorld(transform, name);\n        }\n\n        void Check()\n        {\n            var done = GameState.GetBool(\"Shufen_CommissionDone\");\n            var ng = GameState.GetBool(\"NGPlus\");\n            var key = ng ? \"ngplus\" : (done ? \"hub\" : \"commission\");\n\n            if (!_lastCommissionDone.HasValue || _lastCommissionDone.Value != done)\n            {\n                _lastCommissionDone = done;\n                if (gateBlockCollider != null)\n                    gateBlockCollider.enabled = !done;\n            }\n\n            if (_lastKey == key) return;\n            _lastKey = key;\n            InteractionUtil.SetSpotEnabled(commissionSpot, key == \"commission\");\n            InteractionUtil.SetSpotEnabled(hubSpot, key == \"hub\");\n            InteractionUtil.SetSpotEnabled(ngPlusSpot, key == \"ngplus\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
  }

记录 154 | assistant | 原记录未提供逐条时间
淑芬的 C# 逻辑已经按 Lua 对齐。之前失败是因为场景物体叫 **`淑芬1 / 淑芬2 / 淑芬3`**，代码却在找 `淑芬`，控制器根本没挂上；农场大门是场景根上的 **`HenQuestBlock`**，也没绑到碰撞体。

现在运行时会：

1. 把三只淑芬收到一个专用父物体 `淑芬` 下（不关父物体，只切三个地点）
2. **开局只留 `淑芬1`**，`淑芬2`、`淑芬3` 关掉；`HenQuestBlock` 挡住农场入口
3. 和第一只淑芬把委托说完（`Shufen_CommissionDone`）后：**关掉大门碰撞体**，`淑芬1` 消失，`淑芬2` 出现
4. 案件结束进入二周目（`NGPlus`）后：`淑芬2` 消失，`淑芬3` 出现

请打开 `Mechanics_Code` 直接 Play 验证：先只能在鸡舍口和淑芬说话，进不去农场；对话结束后门应能进，场内换一只淑芬。

记录 183 | assistant | 原记录未提供逐条时间
奶酪碎看得见、走过去却捡不到，是因为 **PC 运行时没有真正接到拾取逻辑**。

场景里的点只是空坐标，奶酪是运行时从预制体生成的。预制体上只有抖音 Lua（`OnPlayerTriggerEnter`），C# 的 `CheesePickup` 从没挂上去。Lua 回调在 PC 上不会触发，所以角色走进去没有任何反应。

现在生成后会立刻挂上 C# 拾取：走进碰撞范围就会加 `CheeseCount`、播音效、隐藏碎片。重新 Play，走到一块奶酪上即可验证。

记录 197 | assistant | 原记录未提供逐条时间
HUD 数字没动，是因为**计数文本没接到脚本上**。场景里数字在笔记本 `Cheese` 图标下的 `Text (Legacy)`，代码却在找名为 `CheeseCount` 的物体，绑定失败，捡奶酪只改了内存变量。

现在会自动找到 `Cheese` 下的数字，第一次捡到后显示并更新计数。重新 Play，捡一块应能看到数字变成 1。
